# Dollhouse source lineage

This index binds 678 complete archival copies to immutable GitHub commits and original Git blob hashes. Code, JSON, HTML and patches are stored as inert .source.md documents. Original receipts and preregistrations retain their historical wording. The catalogue distinguishes installed source, historical specifications and unmerged research; copying does not promote a research candidate.

## Reading the mathematical relationship

Read [FADT](../../FADT.md), [Atlas](../../ATLAS.md), [Pedagogue](../../PEDAGOGUE.md) and [Aperture](../../APERTURE.md) alongside their originals below. The [Aperture mathematical cradle](aperture-mathematical-cradle.md) includes complete embedded doctrine payloads and an inventory of formulations; the archived application remains the byte-authoritative source.

FADT asks when conditioned finite information descends exactly through a retained projection: exact descent is equivalent to support constancy within each occupied projection fibre. Its gap identity and frontier specify what a coarser description loses. Atlas asks complementary reconstruction and custody questions across receiver geometry, incidence, matroid/Tutte, HNF, Schubert, parity and nonabelian structures. Atlas is not wholly derived from FADT alone.

The scientific bridge proceeds through #984 → #986 → #988 → #990 → #992. Subsequent accession is a separate source-custody operation. Independent Pedagogue C14 convergence at #1001 precedes the official Western Horizon rest record at #1002; it is not asserted to be the Git parent.

**REST ≠ empirical completion.** Formal bounded contracts can close while exteriority remains at empirical-shore 𝄐. If twin worlds have I(Ω;A)=0, a deterministic X=f(A) cannot supply new origin information. An independently admitted exogenous witness would need I(Ω;X|A)>0 under its acquisition contract. Bookkeeping, exported packets and CI cannot supply that witness.

Golden Egg remains UNEARNED: its inherited standard requires L≤0.5 bits, R≤0.2, J≤0.1, G empirical binding and C observed matched return on one qualified immutable episode, with the required distinct routes/returns, common frame and custody. The originals below preserve the precise definitions, qualifications and scar ledger.

## Scope and verification

[source-records.json](source-records.json) records commit, original path/blob, byte count and SHA-256 for every copy. The selected families are not a full dependency closure. Each snapshot also links its full immutable repository archive. Historical evaluators/tests are documents here, not installed or executed research. The original Aperture essays are not supplied in full by the embedded excerpts; this index does not invent them.
